Multiplicative Peak Shaving algorithm (MPS):
Input a normalized, beta? For it in range:
   c=conv(a), M=max c
   Choose weighting p: top weights. Could set p_k = (c_k / M)^γ normalized, or excess: p_k = max(c_k - τ,0)^γ normalized, with τ = M - δ*range? Use top m uniformly.
   r = M_p operator on a (via FFT). This is ∂L/∂a.
   We want reduce r large. Multiplicative update: a_i = a_i * exp(-η r_i + η mean?) to maintain sum? If simply normalize after, not need mean. But also set lower floor eps.
   Choose η by line search: try η list, compute score, pick best. This is simple, preserves positivity, time-safe. Could be effective.
But update a_i exp(-η r_i) shrinks positions that contribute to current peaks. Since r_i = Σ p_k a_{k-i}. If p includes peak k, r_i includes a_{k-i}; so positions paired with themselves at peak shrink. This is like applying negative correlation. Then normalize. But if all r positive, it multiplies all by <1; relative changes. This is akin log gradient descent on log a.
Need choose η adaptive. Could use η = c / (||r||_2^2?) For LSE, natural热烈欢迎. Use trial multipliers [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0] and pick improved.
However, if r has high variance, large η may zero out many. Clip to [eps, 1000]. This can sharpen edges. Maybe good.

Could also have "bridge" update: a_i ← a_i / (1 + η r_i) (more stable), normalize. This is like Gauss-Newton. Try both.

Softmax L-BFGS objective function top-level:
```
def lse_softmax_obj(x, beta):
    a = softmax_a(x)
    sc, _ = lse_grad(a, beta)
    return sc
def lse_softmax_jac(x, beta):
    a = softmax_a(x)
    sc, ga = lse_grad(a,beta)
    ga = np.asarray(ga,float)
    dot = float(np.dot(ga, a))
    gy = a * (ga - dot)
    return gy[1:]
```
Need `softmax_a` top-level. But objective and jac recompute a, lse_grad. Could use same function for both.

Let's define concrete helper functions.

`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_sequence(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
if floor>0: arr=np.maximum(arr,floor)
s=arr.sum()
if s <= 1e-12: return np.ones(len(arr))/len(arr)
return arr/s
```
Maybe for interior no floor to allow zeros; for softmax x, no.

`self_conv(a)`:
```
n=len(a); M=next_pow2(2*n-1); A=np.fft.rfft(a,M); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M, n=M)[:2*n-1]
```
Need if A*A may have tiny imaginary? irfft real. Good. Could negative tiny due numerical; score max positive. c sum S^2; if tiny negative at edges not issue for max. For weights c^{q-1}, negative issues; use np.maximum(c,0). For LSE, c negative tiny can make scaled? Use c=np.maximum(c,0) maybe changes sum slightly. Max unaffected. For gradient r, p from c nonnegative. For objective value, maybe use exact c max after clipping? fine.

`compute_score(a)`:
```
a=normalize? For internal we want score of given nonneg no renorm? Use clipping? If a sum<0.01 inf.
n=len(a); c=self_conv(a); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 inf; return 2*n*max(c)/S/S
```
But if a has negatives small, clip. We'll ensure nonneg. For fast many, maybe avoid normalize copy:
```
def score_fast(a):
    S=float(a.sum()); if S<1e-12: return inf
    c=self_conv(a); return 2*len(a)*float(np.max(c))/(S*S)
```

C=Σ p c (not d). Since p from d. Good.

Thus Lq gradient:
- p normalized s^{q-1}
- r = T_p a
- C = dot(p, c)  (or p·c)
- grad = 4n*Lq * (r/S^2 - C/S^3) vector
Objective=2n*Lq.
For q=1, C=S^2/L, r=S/L, grad=0. Good.
For LSE, p from exp, C=p·c, L=LSE(c/S^2)? Wait Ld. For Ld objective, p weights and Ld, grad=4n(r/S^2 - Ld/S) (constant) because C=sum p c = S^2 Ld? Since Ld = p·d? Is p·d = Lq? For LSE, C=p·c = S^2 Ld, so grad=4n Ld (r/S^2 - S^2Ld/S^3)=4n( r/S^2 - Ld/S). same. So generic if C=p·c and L = objective/2n? For both, they are Lq norm of d? For LSE Ld, yes p·d? Does LSE value equal p·d? LSE homogeneous: yes p·d = L? Let's verify: p=softmax, L=m+(1/β)logΣe^{β(d-m)}. p·(d-m)=(1/β)logΣ ... (because dot p = logsum? yes). So p·d=L. Thus C=S^2 L. Good.

Wait for Lq, C = p·c = S^2 (p·d). p·d? For Lq homogeneous? Lq norm not equal p·d with p=s^{q-1}/Σs^{q-1}? p·d = Σd^q/W = Lq^q / W? Not equal Lq generally. Wait for Lq, derivative ∂Lq/∂d_k = (1/q)Lq^{1-q} q d_k^{q-1} = Lq^{1-q} d_k^{q-1}. If p=d^{q-1}/W, coefficient = Lq^{1-q} W p_k. Is that equal Lq p_k? Only if W = Lq^q? But W=Σd^{q-1}, Lq^q=Σd^q. We need full formula.
Let's derive Lq gradient carefully.
F = 2n Lq, Lq=(Σ d^q)^{1/q}.
Derivative ∂Lq/∂d_k = (1/q)Lq^{1-q} q d_k^{q-1} = Lq^{1-q} d_k^{q-1}.
Let w_k = d_k^{q-1}; W=Σw. p=w/W. Then derivative = Lq^{1-q} W p_k.
Call `alpha = Lq^{1-q} * W`.
Then dLq/da_i = alpha * Σ p_k (2a_{k-i}/S^2 - 2c_k/S^3) = 2 alpha (r_i/S^2 - C/S^3), where C=Σp_k c_k.
F gradient =2n*2 alpha (r/S^2 - C/S^3) =4n alpha (r/S^2 - C/S^3).
For q=1, Lq=Σd=1, W=Σw=length L, alpha =1^0 * L =L, C=S^2/L, r=S/L; grad=4nL(S/L/S^2? r/S^2=S/L/S^2=1/(LS); C/S^3=S^2/L/S^3=1/(LS)) zero. Good.
For LSE, Ld is not of form Lq but can be seen with p; alpha? LSE derivative ∂L/∂d_k = L * p_k? Is alpha = L. W? not. So separate functions.

Thus implement Lq gradient:

Let's specify helper functions in detail.

`get_parent_sequence()`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        h = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
        if h is None: return None
        arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
        s=arr.sum()
        if s<1e-9: return None
        return arr/s
    except Exception:
        return None
```

`normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000.0)
    if floor>0: arr=np.maximum(arr,floor)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12:
       if arr.size==0: return np.array([0.5])
       return np.ones_like(arr)/arr.size
    return arr/s
```

`self_conv(a)`:
```
def self_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # Remove tiny negative
    if c.size and c.min() < 0:
       c=np.where(c<0, 0.0, c)
    return c
```
Clipping negatives each time may bias sum slightly; but okay. For gradient, c negative tiny, p weights zero; fine. But clipping c changes ∂c? negligible. Could not clip for score? For weights, c max maybe negative? nonneg a so c nonnumerically; tiny negative okay. We'll clip for safety.

`score_fast(a)`:
```
def score_fast(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2*len(a)*mx/(S*S))
```
`score_conv(a)` returns sc,c,S maybe:
```
def score_and_conv(a):
   S=...
   c=self_conv(a)
   sc=...
   return sc,c,S
```

`weighted_product(p,a)` (T_p a):
```
def weighted_product(p,a):
    n=len(a)
    L=len(p)
    # FFT length for p length L plus n-1
    N=next_pow2(L+n-1)
    z=a[::-1]
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    Z=np.fft.rfft(z,N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(P*Z,N)
    return h[n-1:n+n-1]  # length n? n-1 to n+n-2 inclusive => h[n-1: n+n-1]?
```
Check slice: We need h indices n-1 through 2n-2 inclusive length n. Python slice start=n-1 stop=2n-1. Since stop exclusive `2*n-1`. So `return h[n-1:2*n-1]`. Good.
If p length not 2n-1? For weights yes. Ensure p shape.
Potential optimization: Precompute N? n changes. Fine.
Could use `np.fft.rfft` of length N; if N maybe 0? no.

Endpoint inits:
```
def endpoint_init(n, alpha, rng, add_noise=False):
    i=np.arange(n)
    left=np.power(i+1.0, -alpha)
    right=np.power(n-i, -alpha)
    w=left+right
    w += 0.01*np.min(w) maybe to avoid extremes
    if add_noise: w *= np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
    return normalize_nonneg(w)
```
Try alpha 0.35,0.5,0.7.

Spectral inits:
```
def spectral_inits(n, max_candidates=3):
    inits=[]
    M_opts=unique next_pow(2n-1), next_pow(4n-1) maybe if not too large <=65536? For n=16384, 4n=65536 pow2 okay. For n=12288, 4n=59k next 131k? maybe too big; limit M<=65536 to save.
    for M in M_opts:
       if M > max(65536, 2*(2n-1))? keep.
       T=np.fft.fft(np.concatenate([np.ones(2*n-1), np.zeros(M-(2*n-1))]))
       A=np.zeros(M,complex)
       half=M//2
       for m in range(half+1):
          val=np.sqrt(abs(T[m]))*np.exp(0.5j*np.angle(T[m]))
          A[m]=val
          rm=M-m
          if rm!=m: A[rm]=np.conj(val)
       b=np.fft.ifft(A).real
       for variant in ['pos','abs','posabs']:
           if variant=='pos': arr=np.maximum(b,0.0)
           elif 'abs': arr=np.abs(b)
           else: arr=np.maximum(b,0.0) + 0.1*np.abs(b)
           # choose windows top by sum
           top=window_indices(arr,n,k=max_candidates)
           for s in top:
              win=extract_window(arr,s,n)
              a=normalize_nonneg(win)
              if score finite and sum positive: inits.append(a)
    # maybe also take central window of b? included.
    # deduplicate? not necessary. Score in propose.
    return inits
```
Need no loops? Loops okay. The for m up to M/2 Python loop 32768 maybe okay; but for multiple M/lengths maybe could be 100k iterations, acceptable (<0.1s). Vectorize half:
```
m_idx=np.arange(half+1)
vals=np.sqrt(np.abs(T[m_idx]))*np.exp(0.5j*np.angle(T[m_idx]))
A[m_idx]=vals
A[M - m_idx] = np.conj(vals)
if M%2==0 and M-0? For m=0, M-m = M; need modulo. We can handle:
mirror = (M - m_idx) % M
A[mirror] = np.conj(vals)
# This for m=0 sets A[0]=conj(vals[0]) (same if real? vals0 positive real); okay. For m=half if even, mirror=half, sets itself conj; if complex due numerical, makes real? It overwrites with conj of itself, forcing real. Good. But order: if m_idx includes half and mirror=half, set A[half]=conj(A[half]) -> real part. For other pairs, assigning A[mirror] may overwrite mirror that was also in m_idx? We go vectorized: A[m_idx]=vals first. Then A[mirror]=conj(vals). For mirror values >half not in m_idx, fine. For m=0 mirror 0, overwrites with same. For even half, forces real. But for m where mirror also in m_idx (only 0, half), handled. Good.
```

`window_indices`:
```
def window_score_indices(arr, n, k):
    M=len(arr)
    if M < n: k=min(k,M)
    dup=np.concatenate((arr, arr[:n]))
    cum=np.empty(M+n+1)
    cum[0]=0.0
    cum[1:]=np.cumsum(dup)
    sums=cum[n: n+M] - cum[:M]
    if k >= M: idx=np.arange(M)
    else:
       top=np.argpartition(sums, -k)[-k:]
       # maybe sort top by sum
       order=np.argsort(sums[top])[::-1]
       top=top[order]
    return top
```
`extract_window(arr,start,n)`:
```
M=len(arr)
if start+n <= M: return arr[start:start+n].copy()
else: return np.concatenate((arr[start:], arr[:start+n-M]))
```
For no lambda. Good.

def coordinate_peak_descent(a, t_end, sweeps=2, top_count=40):
   a=normalize; best=a; best_sc=score
   for sweep in range(sweeps):

      k=int(np.argmax(c[0:n]? Actually c length 2n-1; argmax)
      j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
      seg=a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
      if top_count>=len(seg): idx=np.arange(len(seg))
      else: idx=np.argpartition(seg, -top_count)[-top_count:]
      # sort by contrib desc
      idx=idx[np.argsort(seg[idx])[::-1]]

         aj=a[j]
         if aj <= floor: continue
         best_shrink=aj; best_local=best_sc? Maybe compute current score once.

             a[j]=aj*f
             sc=score_fast(a)
             if sc<best_local: ...
         a[j]=best_shrink
      # after sweep normalize
   return best
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`score_and_conv` as:
```
def score_and_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf, np.array([]), 0.0
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf, np.zeros(2*a.size-1), S
    c=self_conv(a)
    mx=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    sc=2*a.size*mx/(S*S)
    return sc,c,S
```

`weighted_product` if p size mismatch? Make p float. `L=len(p)`, if L==0 return zeros.

`lq_score_grad`:
```
def lq_score_grad(a,q):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   S=float(np.sum(a))
   if n==0 or S<1e-12: return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
   c=self_conv(a)
   S2=S*S
   d=c/S2
   dmax=float(np.max(d))
   if dmax <= 0: return 0.0, np.zeros(n) # maybe
   s=d/dmax
   s=np.clip(s, 1e-300, 1.0)  # numerical max >1? clip
   log_s=np.log(s)
   qf=float(q)
   log_t=qf*log_s
   max_t=float(np.max(log_t))
   exp_t=np.exp(log_t - max_t)
   sum_t=float(np.sum(exp_t))
   log_Ls=max_t + np.log(sum_t)
   Ls=np.exp(log_Ls / qf)
   score=2.0*n*dmax*Ls
   if qf <= 1.0:
       p=np.ones(2*n-1)/(2*n-1)
       alpha=2*n-1? But not use.
   else:
       log_w=(qf-1.0)*log_s
       max_w=float(np.max(log_w))
       exp_w=np.exp(log_w - max_w)
       sum_w=float(np.sum(exp_w))
       p=exp_w/sum_w
       log_Ws=max_w + np.log(sum_w)
       log_alpha=log_Ws - (qf-1.0)*np.log(Ls)
       if log_alpha > 700: alpha=np.exp(700)
       elif log_alpha < -745: alpha=0.0
       else: alpha=np.exp(log_alpha)
   r=weighted_product(p,a)
   C=float(np.dot(p,c))
   v=r/(S2) - C/(S2*S)
   grad=4.0*n*alpha*v
   # If grad finite
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros(n)
   if not np.isfinite(score): score=np.inf
   return float(score), grad
```
For q=2, alpha= Ws/Ls^1; Ls=sqrt(Σs^2); Ws=Σs. By Cauchy alpha≥1. okay.
If q very high, sum_w_scaled maybe 1+ small; log_Ws~0; Ls~1; alpha~1.
`p` sums 1 within numerical.

`lse_score_grad`:
```
def lse_score_grad(a,beta):
  ... d,dmax
  b=np.log1p? no scaled=beta*(d-dmax)
  # If beta>0. If beta<=0, p uniform? But objective constant? Return zeros? We won't call beta0.
  exp_s=np.exp(scaled)
  sum_e=np.sum(exp_s)
  if sum_e <=0 or not finite:
     p=np.ones(L)/L
     Lval=dmax
  else:
     p=exp_s/sum_e
     Lval=dmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
  r=weighted_product(p,a)
  C=dot(p,c)
  v=r/S2 - C/(S2*S)
  grad=4.0*n*v
  score=2.0*n*Lval
```
For beta maybe 100000, scaled min -beta*range. range ~1e-5, min -1; okay. For beta 1e6, min -100 underflow; p peaks. Good.

def lq_fun_a(x, q):
    a=np.asarray(x,float)
    if a.min() < 0: a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    sc, grad=lq_score_grad(a, q)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=1e10
    return float(sc), grad
```

`lq_fun_softmax(x,q)`:
```
def lq_fun_softmax(x, q):
    a=softmax_from_x(x)
    sc, grad=lq_score_grad(a,q)
    if not finite: return 1e10, zeros
    dot=float(np.dot(grad,a))
    gy=a*(grad - dot*a)
    if not finite: gy=zeros
    return float(sc), gy[1:]
```
Use `softmax_from_x` top-level. If x contains nan, return 1e10 zeros.
`lse_fun_softmax` similar.

`make_softmax_x(a)`:
```
def make_softmax_x(a):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a, floor=1e-14)
    loga=np.log(a)
    if a.size>0: loga=loga-loga[0]
    return loga[1:]
```

LBFGS runner top-level:
```
def run_lbfgs_q_a(a0, q, t_end):
    now=time.monotonic()
    time_left=t_end-now
    if time_left < 20:
       return score_fast(a0), normalize_nonneg(a0)
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    # benchmark
    bstart=time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3): lq_score_grad(a0,q)
    avg=(time.monotonic()-bstart)/3.0
    if avg < 1e-5: avg=1e-5
    maxfun=int((time_left*0.85)/(avg*8.0))
    maxfun=max(100, min(20000, maxfun))
    maxiter=max(50, maxfun//8)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*len(a0)
    res=minimize(lq_fun_a, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(q,), bounds=bounds, options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50,'disp':False,'m':20})
    a_out=np.clip(res.x,0,1000.0)
    if res.x.sum()<1e-12: a_out=a0
    a_out=normalize_nonneg(a_out)
    sc=score_fast(a_out)
    sc0=score_fast(a0)
    if sc<sc0: return sc,a_out
    return sc0,a0
```

`mps_optimize` implementation in code:
```
def mps_optimize(a0, t_end, specs, rng, floor=1e-12):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fast(a)
    n=len(a)
    total_specs=len(specs)
    for si,(method,param) in enumerate(specs):
        spec_end=min(t_end, time.monotonic() + (t_end - time.monotonic()) / max(1, total_specs - si) + 5.0)
        if time.monotonic() > t_end: break
        eta=0.2
        stale=0
        last_improve=time.monotonic()
        while time.monotonic() < spec_end:
            sc,c,S=score_and_conv(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc): break
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); stale=0
            if S<1e-12: break
            p,C=weights_from_c(c,S,method,param)
            if p is None: break
            r=weighted_product(p,a)
            v=r/(S*S) - C/((S*S)*S)
            u=v - np.mean(v)
            std=float(np.std(u))
            if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-15:
                 # random skip
                 break
            u = u / std
            # adaptive line search
            accepted=False
            for trial_eta in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
                a_trial=multiplicative_trial(a,u,trial_eta,floor)
                if a_trial is None: continue
                sc_t=score_fast(a_trial)
                if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    a=a_trial; eta=min(eta*1.2,0.8); accepted=True; last_improve=time.monotonic(); stale=0
                    break
                elif sc_t < sc - 1e-12:
                    # accept improvement but not best? to make progress
                    a=a_trial; sc=sc_t; if sc<best_sc update; accepted=True; eta=min(eta*1.1,0.8); break
            if not accepted:
                eta=max(eta*0.6, 0.01)
                stale += 1
                if stale > 80:
                    a = randomized_perturb(a, rng, 0.02)
                    stale=0
                    if score_fast(a) < best_sc: update
                    if time.monotonic() > last_improve + max(30.0, (spec_end-last_improve)*0.2): break
        # cap spec time
    return best_a
```
Need `weights_from_c` returns p,C. If all p zeros, uniform.
`randomized_perturb` top-level.
`multiplicative_trial` top-level.

Could be too slow due `score_fast` two times per iter. But okay. Use `sc` from current; for first trial if sc_t<sc update current, else try half. If no, decrease eta. If eta gets <0.005 and stale>30, perturb.

`weights_from_c` top-level:
```
def weights_from_c(c,S,method,param):
   L=len(c); S2=S*S; d=c/S2; dmax=max(d)
   if dmax<=0: return np.ones(L)/L, float(np.sum(c))/L
   if method=='lse':
      beta=float(param)
      if beta <=0: p=ones/L
      else: scaled=beta*(d-dmax); exp; sum; if sum>0 p=exp/sum else uniform
   else: # lq
      q=float(param)
      s=d/dmax; s=np.clip(s,1e-300,1.0); log_s=np.log(s)
      if q<=1: p=ones/L
      else: log_w=(q-1)*log_s; maxw=max; expw; sumw; if sumw>0 p=expw/sumw else uniform
   C=float(np.dot(p,c))
   return p,C
```
For lq q=2, p proportional d. Good.

Implement:
```
def window_score_indices_no_wrap(arr,n,k):
   M=len(arr)
   if M < n: return np.array([],int)
   cum=np.empty(M+1); cum[0]=0; cum[1:]=np.cumsum(arr)
   counts=M-n+1
   sums=cum[n:n+counts]-cum[:counts]
   if k>=counts: idx=np.arange(counts)
   else: top=np.argpartition(sums,-k)[-k:]
       order=np.argsort(sums[top])[::-1]; top=top[order]
   return top.astype(int)
```

def cand_key(item):
    return item[0]

`keep_candidate`:
```
def keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, max_k=8):
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return candidates
    candidates = list(candidates)
    candidates.append((float(sc), int(len(a)), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    candidates.sort(key=cand_key)
    if len(candidates) > max_k:
        candidates = candidates[:max_k]
    return candidates
```

`choose_secondaries`:
```
def choose_secondaries(candidates, best_a, max_count=2):
    res=[]
    for sc,n,a in candidates:
        try:
            same = (len(a)==len(best_a) and len(a)>0 and float(np.max(np.abs(a-best_a))) < 1e-8)
        except Exception:
            same=False
        if not same:
            res.append((sc,n,a))
        if len(res)>=max_count:
            break
    return res
```

`interpolate_to_n`.

`endpoint_init`:
```
def endpoint_init(n, alpha, rng, add_noise=True):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1:
       return np.array([1.0])
    left=np.power(i+1.0, -alpha)
    right=np.power(n-i, -alpha)
    w=left+right
    w+=0.01*np.median(w) if np.median(w)>0 else 1e-6
    if add_noise:
       w = w * np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
    return normalize_nonneg(w, 1e-12)
```
For n large, median >0. If n=1, okay.

Random smooth:
```
def smooth_random_init(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   a=0.5+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)+0.15*np.cos(4*np.pi*x+0.3)
   a += 0.08*rng.standard_normal(n)
   return normalize_nonneg(a,1e-8)
```

def build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_count=8):
   inits=[]
   if parent is not None:
      base=interpolate_to_n(parent,n)
      inits.append(base)
      noisy=base*(1.0+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
      inits.append(normalize_nonneg(noisy,1e-12))
   inits.append(endpoint_init(n,0.35,rng,False))
   inits.append(endpoint_init(n,0.50,rng,False))
   inits.append(endpoint_init(n,0.70,rng,True))
   try:
      sp=spectral_inits(n, max_candidates=3)
      inits.extend(sp[:3])
   except Exception:
      pass
   inits.append(smooth_random_init(n,rng))
   inits.append(np.full(n,0.5))
   return [x for x in inits if x is not None][:max_count]
```

```
def spectral_inits(n,max_candidates=3):
   res=[]
   N=2*n-1
   M_options=[next_pow2(N), next_pow2(4*n-1)]
   # unique <=65536
   seen=set(); M_opts=[]
   for M in M_options:
      if M <= 65536 and M >= N and M not in seen:
         seen.add(M); M_opts.append(M)
   for M in M_opts:
      target=np.zeros(M); target[:N]=1.0
      T=np.fft.fft(target)
      A=np.zeros(M, dtype=np.complex128)
      half=M//2
      idx=np.arange(half+1)
      vals=np.sqrt(np.abs(T[idx])) * np.exp(0.5j*np.angle(T[idx]))
      A[idx]=vals
      mir=(M-idx)%M
      A[mir]=np.conj(vals)
      b=np.fft.ifft(A).real
      variants=[]
      variants.append(('pos', np.maximum(b,0.0)))
      variants.append(('abs', np.abs(b)))
      variants.append(('mix', np.maximum(b,0.0)+0.2*np.abs(b)))
      local=[]
      for _,arr in variants:
         if arr.size < n: continue
         idxs=window_score_indices_no_wrap(arr,n,2)
         for st in idxs:
            win=arr[st:st+n].copy()
            ssum=float(win.sum())
            if ssum > 1e-12:
               local.append(normalize_nonneg(win,0.0))
      # dedupe by maybe score? not; cap
      res.extend(local[:max_candidates*len(M_opts)])
   # return at most maybe max_candidates*2
   return res[:max_candidates*2]
```
Potential: for abs variant, sum high; for pos, maybe many zeros. `window_score_indices_no_wrap` returns empty if M<n. okay.
`T[idx]` with idx array; but if M=1, half=0, idx [0], mir[0], okay.

`window_score_indices_no_wrap`:
```
def window_score_indices_no_wrap(arr, n, k):
    M=arr.size
    if M < n or k <=0:
       return np.array([], dtype=int)
    counts=M-n+1
    cum=np.empty(M+1, dtype=np.float64)
    cum[0]=0.0
    cum[1:]=np.cumsum(arr)
    sums=cum[n:n+counts]-cum[:counts]
    if k >= counts:
       return np.arange(counts, dtype=int)
    top=np.argpartition(sums, -k)[-k:]
    order=np.argsort(sums[top])[::-1]
    return top[order].astype(int)
```

`extract_window` not needed.

        grad=np.zeros_like(a)

`Ls = exp(log_Ls/qf)`; if log_Ls/qf >700? log_Ls≤logL ~9, no.
`log_Ws` ≤logL, no. `log_alpha=log_Ws - (q-1)*log(Ls)`. Could be negative large? For flat q high, log_Ws=logL=9, log_Ls=(1/q)logL=0.14, (q-1)*logLs≈8.8, alpha≈1. For unique peak, Ws=1, Ls=1 alpha=1. So log_alpha small [-?]. safe. But for some distributions maybe Ws small? Since s max 1, Ws≥1 (the max s=1 term => s^{q-1}=1), log_Ws≥0. Ls≥1, log_alpha≤0? Since Ws≤Ls^{q}? Not. Now, coordinate descent function top-level:
```
def coordinate_peak_descent(a0,t_end, sweeps=2, top_count=30):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a0); best=a; best_sc=score_fast(a)
   if time.monotonic() > t_end: return best_sc,best
   for sweep in range(sweeps):
      if time.monotonic() > t_end - 5: break
      sc,c,S=score_and_conv(a)
      if not np.isfinite(sc): break
      k=int(np.argmax(c))
      n=len(a)
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
      seg=a[js:je+1][::-1].copy()
      if top_count >= len(seg): idx=np.arange(len(seg))
      else:
         idx=np.argpartition(seg, -top_count)[-top_count:]
         idx=idx[np.argsort(seg[idx])[::-1]]
      for loc in idx:
         j=js+int(loc)
         aj=float(a[j])
         if aj < 1e-14: continue
         cur_sc=best_sc? use current score? We'll compute trial scores and keep local best from aj.
         local_best=aj; local_sc=best_sc
         for f in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5):
             a[j]=aj*f
             sc_t=score_fast(a)
             if sc_t < local_sc:
                 local_sc=sc_t; local_best=aj*f
         a[j]=local_best
         if local_sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=local_sc; best=a.copy()
      # after sweep normalize
      a=normalize_nonneg(a)
      sc=score_fast(a)
      if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
   return best_sc,best
```
This recomputes score for each factor; if f=1? not. Use factors maybe skip if no improvement. `best_sc` may be lower than sc after previous variable; local baseline should be current score? If best_sc lower than current due previous, but current a after previous modifications maybe score not best? We update a to local best each variable, so current score should be <= previous. Use `base_sc=score_fast(a)` before trying each variable? That adds cost. We can set `local_sc = best_sc` and if any trial better update; if none, leave aj. If current a score already worse than best_sc (shouldn't if we accept only improvements), maybe trial factors never beat best but could improve current; not tracked. To be safer, compute `cur_sc=score_fast(a)` before each variable? That is n_top extra evals. Acceptable? 30*2=60. But factor tries 5*30=150; one extra okay. Use `cur_sc = score_fast(a)` before variable modifications; local_sc=cur_sc. Then if improvements, update. After sweep, normalize and score.
```
cur_sc=score_fast(a)
if cur_sc<best_sc: best...
for loc:
  aj=...
  local_best=aj; local_sc=cur_sc
  for f:
    a[j]=aj*f
    sc_t=score_fast(a)
    if sc_t < local_sc: local_sc=sc_t; local_best=aj*f
  a[j]=local_best
  cur_sc=local_sc
```

def propose_candidate(seed=2024, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+total-60.0
    parent=get_parent_sequence()
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    candidates=[]
    if parent is not None:
        a=normalize_nonneg(parent); sc=score_fast(a); best_a=a; best_sc=sc; candidates=keep_candidate(candidates,a,sc,8); print
    # If no parent, initialize best with constant length 8192? We'll set after lengths.
    lengths=[]
    if parent is not None: lengths.append(len(parent))
    for n in (4096,8192,12288,16384): lengths.append(n)
    lengths=sorted(set(lengths))
    quick_end=min(deadline, t0+180.0)
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic() > quick_end - 30: break
       print length
       inits=build_initializations(n,parent,rng,max_count=8)
       for a0 in inits:
          if time.monotonic() > quick_end - 10: break
          a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
          sc=score_fast(a)
          if np.isfinite(sc):
             candidates=keep_candidate(candidates,a,sc,8)
             if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); print
    if best_a is None:
       n=8192 if parent is None else parent.shape[0]
       best_a=interpolate_to_n(np.ones(4096), n) # or endpoint
       best_sc=score_fast(best_a)
    print after quick.
    # Primary MPS
    mps_end_primary=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+200.0)
    specs_primary=[('lq',4.0), ('lq',16.0), ('lse',2000.0), ('lse',50000.0)]
    a=mps_optimize(best_a, mps_end_primary, specs_primary, rng)
    sc=score_fast(a); update
    candidates=keep_candidate(candidates,a,sc,8)
    # Primary LBFGS tasks with remaining reserve for secondary/final
    secondary_reserve=350.0
    final_reserve=250.0
    primary_end=min(deadline, time.monotonic() + (deadline - time.monotonic() - secondary_reserve - final_reserve))
    # But if negative, primary_end=now.
    if primary_end > time.monotonic() + 30:
       tasks=[('q',8.0,0.05), ('q',16.0,0.10), ('q',32.0,0.20), ('q',64.0,0.25), ('lse',50000.0,0.15), ('soft_q',32.0,0.25)]
       total_weight=sum(w for ...)
       remain_time=primary_end-time.monotonic()
       for kind,param,weight in tasks:
          if time.monotonic() > primary_end - 20: break
          budget=max(20.0, remain_time*weight/total_weight)
          task_end=min(primary_end, time.monotonic()+budget)
          if kind=='q': sc,a=run_lbfgs_q_a(best_a,param, task_end)
          elif kind=='lse': sc,a=run_lbfgs_lse_a(best_a,param, task_end)
          elif kind=='soft_q': sc,a=run_lbfgs_q_softmax(best_a,param, task_end)
          update; keep
    # Secondary candidates
    sec_end=min(deadline, deadline - final_reserve)
    secondaries=choose_secondaries(candidates,best_a,2)
    # If not enough, add interpolated length e.g. 8192/16384
    if not secondaries:
       n_alt=8192 if len(best_a)!=8192 else 16384
       if n_alt != len(best_a): secondaries=[(best_sc*1.01,n_alt,interpolate_to_n(best_a,n_alt))]
    for idx,(sc0,n,a0) in enumerate(secondaries[:2]):
       if time.monotonic() > sec_end - 60: break
       # low MPS 60s
       mps_end=min(sec_end, time.monotonic()+80.0)
       a2=mps_optimize(a0, mps_end, [('lq',16.0),('lse',20000.0)], rng)
       sc2=score_fast(a2); update
       # LBFGS q32 90s, q64 90s
       t_end=min(sec_end, time.monotonic()+120.0)
       sc2,a2=run_lbfgs_q_a(a2,32.0,t_end)
       update
       if time.monotonic() < sec_end - 60:
           t_end=min(sec_end, time.monotonic()+90.0)
           sc2,a2=run_lbfgs_q_a(a2,64.0,t_end)
           update
    # Final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
       # coordinate descent budget 80
       cd_end=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+80)
       sc_cd,a_cd=coordinate_peak_descent(best_a, cd_end, sweeps=2, top_count=20)
       update
       # LBFGS tasks on final best: q64 100, lse high 100, soft_q64 100 if time
       tasks_final=[('q',64.0,0.3),('lse',200000.0,0.3),('soft_q',64.0,0.4)]
       total_weight=1.0
       final_time=max(0, deadline-time.monotonic())
       for ...:
          budget=final_time*w; task_end=now+budget
          run
    # Final verification and return
    out=...
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function height sequence minimizing

    score = 2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using a hybrid of:
  * informative initializers (endpoint-singular shapes, spectral square-root
    of a flat convolution target, parent interpolation, smooth random);
  * adaptive multiplicative peak-shaving using Lq / log-sum-exp weights;
  * L-BFGS on the simplex (plain non-negative variables and softmax variables)
    with annealed Lq and LSE smoothings;
  * final coordinate peak shaving.

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1 and clipped to [0, 1000].
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# small utilities
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, max_k=8):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return candidates
    candidates = list(candidates)
    candidates.append((float(sc), int(len(a)), np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    candidates.sort(key=cand_key)
    if len(candidates) > max_k:
        candidates = candidates[:max_k]
    return candidates


def choose_secondaries(candidates, best_a, max_count=2):
    res = []
    for sc, n, a in candidates:
        try:
            same = (
                len(a) == len(best_a)
                and len(a) > 0
                and float(np.max(np.abs(a - best_a))) < 1e-8
            )
        except Exception:
            same = False
        if not same:
            res.append((float(sc), int(n), a))
        if len(res) >= max_count:
            break
    return res


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        arr = np.maximum(arr, float(floor))
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 1e-9:
            return None
        return arr / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def interpolate_to_n(parent, n):
    if parent is None:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if parent.size == n:
        return parent.copy()
    if parent.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    if parent.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(parent[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent).astype(np.float64)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# convolution / scoring / smooth gradients
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m_len = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(m_len)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:m_len]
    if c.size and float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
        c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if float(np.min(a)) < 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mx / (S * S))


def score_and_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    if float(np.min(a)) < 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(2 * a.size - 1, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    c = self_conv(a)
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    sc = float(2.0 * a.size * mx / (S * S))
    return sc, c, S


def weighted_product(p, a):
    """
    Compute (M(p) a)_i = sum_j p_{i+j} a_j for i=0..n-1.
    p has length 2n-1.  Uses FFT via reversed a.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_pow2(L + n - 1)
    z = a[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    Z = np.fft.rfft(z, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(P * Z, N)
    return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def weights_from_c(c, S, method, param):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = c.size
    if L == 0 or S < 1e-12:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    S2 = S * S
    d = c / S2
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        return p, float(np.dot(p, c))

    if method == "lse":
        beta = float(param)
        if beta <= 1e-8:
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            scaled = beta * (d - dmax)
            e = np.exp(scaled)
            se = float(np.sum(e))
            if se <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(se):
                p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
            else:
                p = e / se
    else:  # Lq-like weights, p_k ~ d_k^(q-1)
        q = float(param)
        if q <= 1.0:
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            s = d / dmax
            s = np.clip(s, 1e-300, 1.0)
            log_s = np.log(s)
            log_w = (q - 1.0) * log_s
            maxw = float(np.max(log_w))
            ew = np.exp(log_w - maxw)
            sw = float(np.sum(ew))
            if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
            else:
                p = ew / sw

    C = float(np.dot(p, c))
    return p, C


def lq_score_grad(a, q):
    """
    Lq-smoothed surrogate:
       F = 2n * (sum_k (c_k / S^2)^q)^(1/q)
    Returns (F, gradient wrt a).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if float(np.min(a)) < 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = self_conv(a)
    S2 = S * S
    d = c / S2
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    qf = float(q)
    s = np.clip(d / dmax, 1e-300, 1.0)
    log_s = np.log(s)

    if qf <= 1.0:
        Ls = 1.0
        p = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        alpha = float(c.size)
    else:
        log_t = qf * log_s
        max_t = float(np.max(log_t))
        e_t = np.exp(log_t - max_t)
        sum_t = float(np.sum(e_t))
        log_Ls = max_t + np.log(sum_t)
        Ls = np.exp(log_Ls / qf)

        log_w = (qf - 1.0) * log_s
        max_w = float(np.max(log_w))
        e_w = np.exp(log_w - max_w)
        sum_w = float(np.sum(e_w))

        if sum_w <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sum_w):
            p = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
            log_Ws = 0.0
        else:
            p = e_w / sum_w
            log_Ws = max_w + np.log(sum_w)

        log_alpha = log_Ws - (qf - 1.0) * np.log(Ls)
        if log_alpha > 700.0:
            alpha = float(np.exp(700.0))
        elif log_alpha < -745.0:
            alpha = 0.0
        else:
            alpha = float(np.exp(log_alpha))

    r = weighted_product(p, a)
    C = float(np.dot(p, c))
    v = r / S2 - C / (S2 * S)
    grad = 4.0 * n * alpha * v

    score = 2.0 * n * dmax * Ls

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(score), grad


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    """
    Log-sum-exp smoothed surrogate:
       F = 2n * LSE(c/S^2)
    Uses the correct scale-invariant gradient.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if float(np.min(a)) < 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = self_conv(a)
    S2 = S * S
    d = c / S2
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        p = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        Lval = float(np.mean(d))
    else:
        scaled = beta * (d - dmax)
        e = np.exp(scaled)
        se = float(np.sum(e))
        if se <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(se):
            p = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
            Lval = dmax
        else:
            p = e / se
            Lval = dmax + np.log(se) / beta

    r = weighted_product(p, a)
    C = float(np.dot(p, c))
    v = r / S2 - C / (S2 * S)
    grad = 4.0 * n * v

    score = 2.0 * n * Lval

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(score), grad


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# optimization wrappers
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def lq_fun_a(x, q):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if float(np.min(arr)) < 0.0:
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    sc, g = lq_score_grad(arr, q)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = 1e10
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), g


def lse_fun_a(x, beta):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if float(np.min(arr)) < 0.0:
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    sc, g = lse_score_grad(arr, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = 1e10
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), g


def softmax_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0] = 0.0
    if n > 1:
        y[1:] = x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y -= float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def make_softmax_x(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a, floor=1e-14)
    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    loga = np.log(a)
    loga = loga - loga[0]
    return loga[1:].astype(np.float64)


def lq_fun_softmax(x, q):
    arr_x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr_x)):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr_x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = softmax_from_x(arr_x)
    sc, g = lq_score_grad(a, q)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr_x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    dot = float(np.dot(g, a))
    gy = a * (g - dot * a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        gy = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), gy[1:]


def lse_fun_softmax(x, beta):
    arr_x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr_x)):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr_x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = softmax_from_x(arr_x)
    sc, g = lse_score_grad(a, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(arr_x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    dot = float(np.dot(g, a))
    gy = a * (g - dot * a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
        gy = np.zeros(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(sc), gy[1:]


def _estimate_maxfun(time_left, avg_eval_time, cap=20000):
    if avg_eval_time < 1e-6:
        avg_eval_time = 1e-6
    maxfun = int((time_left * 0.85) / (avg_eval_time * 8.0))
    maxfun = max(80, min(cap, maxfun))
    return maxfun


def run_lbfgs_q_a(a0, q, t_end):
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = float(t_end - now)
    if remaining < 25.0:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    # small benchmark to choose a safe maxfun
    bstart = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        lq_score_grad(a0, q)
    avg = (time.monotonic() - bstart) / 3.0

    maxfun = _estimate_maxfun(remaining, avg, cap=22000)
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 8)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * a0.size
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 50,
        "disp": False,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lq_fun_a,
            a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(q),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        a_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a_out = np.clip(a_out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(a_out.sum()) < 1e-9:
            a_out = a0
        a_out = normalize_nonneg(a_out)
        sc = score_fast(a_out)
        sc0 = score_fast(a0)
        if sc < sc0:
            return float(sc), a_out
        return float(sc0), a0
    except Exception:
        return score_fast(a0), a0


def run_lbfgs_lse_a(a0, beta, t_end):
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = float(t_end - now)
    if remaining < 25.0:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    bstart = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        lse_score_grad(a0, beta)
    avg = (time.monotonic() - bstart) / 3.0

    maxfun = _estimate_maxfun(remaining, avg, cap=22000)
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 8)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * a0.size
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 50,
        "disp": False,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_fun_a,
            a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        a_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a_out = np.clip(a_out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(a_out.sum()) < 1e-9:
            a_out = a0
        a_out = normalize_nonneg(a_out)
        sc = score_fast(a_out)
        sc0 = score_fast(a0)
        if sc < sc0:
            return float(sc), a_out
        return float(sc0), a0
    except Exception:
        return score_fast(a0), a0


def run_lbfgs_q_softmax(a0, q, t_end):
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a0.size <= 1:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = float(t_end - now)
    if remaining < 25.0:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    x0 = make_softmax_x(a0)
    bstart = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        lq_fun_softmax(x0, q)
    avg = (time.monotonic() - bstart) / 3.0

    maxfun = _estimate_maxfun(remaining, avg, cap=18000)
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 12)

    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
        "disp": False,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lq_fun_softmax,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(q),),
            bounds=None,
            options=opts,
        )
        x_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a_out = softmax_from_x(x_out)
        a_out = normalize_nonneg(a_out)
        sc = score_fast(a_out)
        sc0 = score_fast(a0)
        if sc < sc0:
            return float(sc), a_out
        return float(sc0), a0
    except Exception:
        return score_fast(a0), a0


def run_lbfgs_lse_softmax(a0, beta, t_end):
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a0.size <= 1:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = float(t_end - now)
    if remaining < 25.0:
        return score_fast(a0), a0

    x0 = make_softmax_x(a0)
    bstart = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        lse_fun_softmax(x0, beta)
    avg = (time.monotonic() - bstart) / 3.0

    maxfun = _estimate_maxfun(remaining, avg, cap=18000)
    maxiter = max(50, maxfun // 12)

    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 40,
        "disp": False,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_fun_softmax,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),),
            bounds=None,
            options=opts,
        )
        x_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a_out = softmax_from_x(x_out)
        a_out = normalize_nonneg(a_out)
        sc = score_fast(a_out)
        sc0 = score_fast(a0)
        if sc < sc0:
            return float(sc), a_out
        return float(sc0), a0
    except Exception:
        return score_fast(a0), a0


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# multiplicative peak-shaving
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def multiplicative_trial(a, u, eta, floor=1e-14):
    delta = -float(eta) * u
    delta = np.clip(delta, -4.0, 4.0)
    out = a * np.exp(delta)
    out = np.maximum(out, float(floor))
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return out / s


def randomized_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.03):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    noise = rng.standard_normal(a.size) * float(strength)
    out = a * (1.0 + noise)
    return normalize_nonneg(out, 1e-12)


def mps_optimize(a0, t_end, specs, rng, floor=1e-14):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_fast(a)

    total_specs = len(specs)
    if total_specs == 0:
        return best_a

    for si, (method, param) in enumerate(specs):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_end:
            break
        remaining_specs = max(1, total_specs - si)
        spec_end = min(float(t_end), now + (float(t_end) - now) / float(remaining_specs) + 2.0)

        eta = 0.25
        stale = 0
        last_improve = time.monotonic()

        while time.monotonic() < spec_end:
            sc, c, S = score_and_conv(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc) or S < 1e-12:
                break

            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                stale = 0

            p, C = weights_from_c(c, S, method, param)
            if p is None or p.size == 0:
                break

            r = weighted_product(p, a)
            v = r / (S * S) - C / ((S * S) * S)
            u = v - float(np.mean(v))
            std = float(np.std(u))
            if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-15:
                break
            u = u / std

            accepted = False
            for trial_eta in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta):
                if trial_eta <= 1e-6:
                    continue
                a_trial = multiplicative_trial(a, u, trial_eta, floor)
                if a_trial is None:
                    continue
                sc_t = score_fast(a_trial)
                if np.isfinite(sc_t):
                    if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        a = a_trial
                        best_sc = sc_t
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        stale = 0
                        eta = min(eta * 1.15, 0.85)
                        accepted = True
                        break
                    elif sc_t < sc - 1e-12:
                        a = a_trial
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        stale = 0
                        eta = min(eta * 1.05, 0.85)
                        accepted = True
                        break

            if not accepted:
                eta = max(eta * 0.65, 0.008)
                stale += 1
                if stale > 70 and eta < 0.03:
                    a = randomized_perturb(a, rng, 0.035)
                    sc_p = score_fast(a)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_p) and sc_p < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc_p
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                    stale = 0
                    eta = 0.12

                if time.monotonic() - last_improve > 45.0 and stale > 140:
                    break

    final_sc = score_fast(a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinate peak shaving
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def coordinate_peak_descent(a0, t_end, sweeps=2, top_count=24):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_fast(a)
    n = a.size

    for _ in range(int(sweeps)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(t_end) - 5.0:
            break

        sc, c, S = score_and_conv(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or S < 1e-12 or c.size == 0:
            break

        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(k, n - 1)
        seg = a[js:je + 1][::-1].copy()
        m = seg.size
        if m == 0:
            break

        if top_count >= m:
            idx = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(seg, -int(top_count))[-int(top_count):]
            idx = idx[np.argsort(seg[idx])[::-1]]

        cur_sc = score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        for loc in idx:
            j = js + int(loc)
            aj = float(a[j])
            if aj <= 1e-14:
                continue

            local_best = aj
            local_sc = cur_sc
            for f in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.6):
                a[j] = aj * float(f)
                sct = score_fast(a)
                if np.isfinite(sct) and sct < local_sc:
                    local_sc = sct
                    local_best = aj * float(f)
            a[j] = local_best
            cur_sc = local_sc

            if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_a = a.copy()

        a = normalize_nonneg(a)
        sc_end = score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_end) and sc_end < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_end
            best_a = a.copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# initializers
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def endpoint_init(n, alpha=0.5, rng=None, add_noise=True):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.power(i + 1.0, -float(alpha))
    right = np.power(float(n) - i, -float(alpha))
    w = left + right
    med = float(np.median(w))
    w += 0.01 * (med if med > 0.0 else 1e-6)
    if add_noise and rng is not None:
        w = w * np.exp(0.10 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    return normalize_nonneg(w, 1e-12)


def smooth_random_init(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 0.5 + 0.30 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.15 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x + 0.35)
    a += 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return normalize_nonneg(a, 1e-8)


def window_score_indices_no_wrap(arr, n, k):
    M = arr.size
    if M < n or k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    counts = M - n + 1
    cum = np.empty(M + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cum[0] = 0.0
    cum[1:] = np.cumsum(arr)
    sums = cum[n:n + counts] - cum[:counts]

    if k >= counts:
        return np.arange(counts, dtype=int)

    top = np.argpartition(sums, -int(k))[-int(k):]
    order = np.argsort(sums[top])[::-1]
    return top[order].astype(int)


def spectral_inits(n, max_candidates=3):
    res = []
    N = 2 * n - 1
    options = [next_pow2(N), next_pow2(4 * n - 1)]
    seen = set()
    Ms = []
    for M in options:
        if M >= N and M <= 65536 and M not in seen:
            seen.add(M)
            Ms.append(M)

    for M in Ms:
        target = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        target[:N] = 1.0
        T = np.fft.fft(target)

        A = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.complex128)
        half = M // 2
        idx = np.arange(half + 1, dtype=int)
        vals = np.sqrt(np.abs(T[idx])) * np.exp(0.5j * np.angle(T[idx]))
        A[idx] = vals
        mir = (M - idx) % M
        A[mir] = np.conj(vals)

        b = np.fft.ifft(A).real

        variants = []
        variants.append(np.maximum(b, 0.0))
        variants.append(np.abs(b))
        variants.append(np.maximum(b, 0.0) + 0.20 * np.abs(b))

        local = []
        for arr in variants:
            if arr.size < n:
                continue
            idxs = window_score_indices_no_wrap(arr, n, 2)
            for st in idxs:
                win = arr[st:st + n].copy()
                ssum = float(win.sum())
                if ssum > 1e-12:
                    local.append(normalize_nonneg(win, 0.0))

        res.extend(local[:max(1, 2 * max_candidates)])

    return res[:max(1, 2 * max_candidates)]


def build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_count=8):
    inits = []

    if parent is not None:
        base = interpolate_to_n(parent, n)
        inits.append(base)
        noisy = base * (1.0 + 0.10 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        inits.append(normalize_nonneg(noisy, 1e-12))

    inits.append(endpoint_init(n, 0.35, rng, False))
    inits.append(endpoint_init(n, 0.50, rng, False))
    inits.append(endpoint_init(n, 0.70, rng, True))

    try:
        sp = spectral_inits(n, max_candidates=3)
        inits.extend(sp[:3])
    except Exception:
        pass

    inits.append(smooth_random_init(n, rng))
    inits.append(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64))

    out = []
    for arr in inits:
        try:
            a = normalize_nonneg(arr)
            if a.size == n and np.isfinite(score_fast(a)):
                out.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if len(out) >= max_count:
            break
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------
# main search
# ------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=20240607, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    reserve = 60.0 if total > 120.0 else max(1.0, 0.10 * total)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, total - reserve)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    candidates = []

    def current_time():
        return time.monotonic()

    # initial parent
    if parent is not None:
        a = normalize_nonneg(parent)
        sc = score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, 8)
            print(f"[init parent] n={a.size} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # length list
    lengths = []
    if parent is not None and 512 <= parent.size <= 16384:
        lengths.append(parent.size)
    for nn in (4096, 8192, 12288, 16384):
        lengths.append(nn)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    # ---------------- quick diverse initial search ----------------
    quick_end = min(deadline, t0 + 180.0)
    print(f"\nQuick init phase until t={quick_end - t0:.0f}s")

    for nn in lengths:
        if current_time() > quick_end - 20.0:
            break
        print(f"  building inits n={nn}")
        inits = build_initializations(nn, parent, rng, max_count=8)
        for a0 in inits:
            if current_time() > quick_end - 5.0:
                break
            a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
            sc = score_fast(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, 8)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"    [init n={nn}] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f}")

    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        n_fb = parent.size if (parent is not None and 512 <= parent.size <= 16384) else 8192
        best_a = endpoint_init(n_fb, 0.5, rng, False)
        best_sc = score_fast(best_a)
        candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, best_a, best_sc, 8)
        print(f"fallback init score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # ---------------- primary multiplicative peak shaving ----------------
    mps_primary_end = min(deadline, current_time() + 220.0)
    specs_primary = [
        ("lq", 4.0),
        ("lq", 16.0),
        ("lse", 2000.0),
        ("lse", 50000.0),
    ]
    best_a = mps_optimize(best_a, mps_primary_end, specs_primary, rng)
    best_sc = score_fast(best_a)
    candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, best_a, best_sc, 8)
    print(f"After primary MPS: {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size} t={current_time()-t0:.0f}s")

    # ---------------- primary LBFGS tasks ----------------
    secondary_reserve = 320.0
    final_reserve = 280.0
    remain_until_primary = max(0.0, deadline - current_time() - secondary_reserve - final_reserve)
    primary_end = current_time() + remain_until_primary

    tasks_primary = [
        ("q", 8.0, 0.05),
        ("q", 16.0, 0.10),
        ("q", 32.0, 0.20),
        ("q", 64.0, 0.25),
        ("lse", 50000.0, 0.15),
        ("soft_q", 32.0, 0.25),
    ]

    if primary_end > current_time() + 25.0:
        total_w = 0.0
        for _, _, w in tasks_primary:
            total_w += float(w)

        rem_primary = primary_end - current_time()
        for kind, param, w in tasks_primary:
            if current_time() > primary_end - 20.0:
                break
            budget = max(25.0, rem_primary * (float(w) / total_w))
            task_end = min(primary_end, current_time() + budget)

            if kind == "q":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_q_a(best_a, float(param), task_end)
            elif kind == "lse":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_lse_a(best_a, float(param), task_end)
            elif kind == "soft_q":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_q_softmax(best_a, float(param), task_end)
            elif kind == "soft_lse":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_lse_softmax(best_a, float(param), task_end)
            else:
                continue

            a = normalize_nonneg(a)
            sc = score_fast(a)
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, 8)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"  [{kind} {param}] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size} t={current_time()-t0:.0f}s")
            else:
                print(f"  [{kind} {param}] best remains {best_sc:.10f} t={current_time()-t0:.0f}s")

    # ---------------- secondary candidates ----------------
    secondary_end = min(deadline, deadline - final_reserve)
    secondaries = choose_secondaries(candidates, best_a, max_count=2)

    if len(secondaries) < 2:
        for nn in (8192, 16384, 4096):
            if len(secondaries) >= 2:
                break
            if nn == best_a.size:
                continue
            a_alt = interpolate_to_n(best_a, nn)
            sc_alt = score_fast(a_alt)
            secondaries.append((sc_alt, nn, a_alt))

    for i, (_, _, a0) in enumerate(secondaries[:2]):
        if current_time() > secondary_end - 90.0:
            break

        a_s = normalize_nonneg(a0)
        # skip if identical to current best
        if a_s.size == best_a.size and np.allclose(a_s, best_a, atol=1e-9):
            continue

        mps_end = min(secondary_end, current_time() + 80.0)
        a_s = mps_optimize(a_s, mps_end, [("lq", 16.0), ("lse", 20000.0)], rng)
        sc_s = score_fast(a_s)
        candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a_s, sc_s, 8)
        if sc_s < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_s
            best_a = a_s.copy()
            print(f"  [secondary {i} after MPS] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

        if current_time() < secondary_end - 90.0:
            te = min(secondary_end, current_time() + 120.0)
            sc_s, a_s = run_lbfgs_q_a(a_s, 32.0, te)
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a_s, sc_s, 8)
            if sc_s < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_s
                best_a = a_s.copy()
                print(f"  [secondary {i} q32] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

        if current_time() < secondary_end - 70.0:
            te = min(secondary_end, current_time() + 100.0)
            sc_s, a_s = run_lbfgs_q_a(a_s, 64.0, te)
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a_s, sc_s, 8)
            if sc_s < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_s
                best_a = a_s.copy()
                print(f"  [secondary {i} q64] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ---------------- final polish ----------------
    if current_time() < deadline - 35.0:
        cd_end = min(deadline, current_time() + 90.0)
        sc_cd, a_cd = coordinate_peak_descent(best_a, cd_end, sweeps=2, top_count=22)
        candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a_cd, sc_cd, 8)
        if sc_cd < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_cd
            best_a = a_cd.copy()
            print(f"  [coord] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    final_window = max(0.0, deadline - current_time() - 20.0)
    if final_window > 30.0:
        final_tasks = [
            ("q", 64.0, 0.35),
            ("lse", 200000.0, 0.30),
            ("soft_q", 64.0, 0.35),
        ]
        total_w = 0.0
        for _, _, w in final_tasks:
            total_w += float(w)

        for kind, param, w in final_tasks:
            if current_time() > deadline - 25.0:
                break
            budget = max(20.0, final_window * (float(w) / total_w))
            te = min(deadline - 5.0, current_time() + budget)

            if kind == "q":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_q_a(best_a, float(param), te)
            elif kind == "lse":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_lse_a(best_a, float(param), te)
            elif kind == "soft_q":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_q_softmax(best_a, float(param), te)
            elif kind == "soft_lse":
                sc, a = run_lbfgs_lse_softmax(best_a, float(param), te)
            else:
                continue

            a = normalize_nonneg(a)
            sc = score_fast(a)
            candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a, sc, 8)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"  [final {kind} {param}] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ---------------- very last short coordinate sweep if time ----------------
    if current_time() < deadline - 35.0:
        cd_end = min(deadline - 5.0, current_time() + 60.0)
        sc_cd, a_cd = coordinate_peak_descent(best_a, cd_end, sweeps=1, top_count=16)
        candidates = keep_candidate(candidates, a_cd, sc_cd, 8)
        if sc_cd < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_cd
            best_a = a_cd.copy()
            print(f"  [last coord] NEW BEST {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ---------------- output ----------------
    best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a)
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a.tolist()]

    try:
        if sum(out) < 0.01:
            out = [0.5] * 64
            verified = evaluate_sequence(out)
        else:
            verified = evaluate_sequence(out)
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc
        if sum(out) < 0.01:
            out = [0.5] * 64
            try:
                verified = evaluate_sequence(out)
            except Exception:
                verified = best_sc

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f} verified={verified:.10f} n={len(out)} time={elapsed:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
